```
1→"""Subjective code review: context building, file selection, and issue import.
     2→
     3→Desloppify prepares structured review data (context + file batches + prompts)
     4→for an AI agent to evaluate. The agent returns structured issues that are
     5→imported back into state like any other detector.
     6→
     7→No LLM calls happen here — this module is pure Python.
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from typing import Any
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    14→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts_types import ReviewImportPayload
    15→
    16→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
```

```
1→"""Prompt template helpers for holistic review batch subagents."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
     8→    DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY,
     9→    HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD,
    10→)
    11→
    12→from ..prompt_sections import (
    13→    PromptBatchContext,
    14→    build_batch_context,
    15→    join_non_empty_sections,
    16→    render_historical_focus,
    17→    render_mechanical_concern_signals,
    18→    render_scan_evidence_note,
    19→    render_scoring_frame,
    20→    render_scope_enums,
    21→    render_seed_files_block,
    22→    render_task_requirements,
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def _render_metadata_block(
    27→    *,
    28→    repo_root: Path,
    29→    packet_path: Path,
    30→    batch_index: int,
    31→    context: PromptBatchContext,
    32→) -> str:
    33→    return (
    34→        "You are a focused subagent reviewer for a single holistic investigation batch.\n\n"
    35→        f"Repository root: {repo_root}\n"
    36→        f"Blind packet: {packet_path}\n"
    37→        f"Batch index: {batch_index + 1}\n"
    38→        f"Batch name: {context.name}\n"
    39→        f"Batch dimensions: {context.dimensions_text}\n"
    40→        f"Batch rationale: {context.rationale}\n\n"
    41→    )
    42→
    43→
    44→def _render_output_schema(context: PromptBatchContext, batch_index: int) -> str:
    45→    return (
    46→        "Output schema:\n"
    47→        "{\n"
    48→        f'  "batch": "{context.name}",\n'
    49→        f'  "batch_index": {batch_index + 1},\n'
    50→        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100 with one decimal place>},\n'
    51→        '  "dimension_notes": {\n'
    52→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
    53→        '      "evidence": ["specific code observations"],\n'
    54→        '      "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
    55→        '      "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    56→        '      "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
    57→        f'      "{DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY}": "required when score >{HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f}",\n'
    58→        '      "sub_axes": {"abstraction_leverage": 0-100, "indirection_cost": 0-100, "interface_honesty": 0-100, "delegation_density": 0-100, "definition_directness": 0-100, "type_discipline": 0-100}  // required for abstraction_fitness when evidence supports it; all one decimal place\n'
    59→        "    }\n"
    60→        "  },\n"
    61→        '  "issues": [{\n'
    62→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
    63→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
    64→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
    65→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
    66→        '    "evidence": ["specific code observation"],\n'
    67→        '    "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",\n'
    68→        '    "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
    69→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
    70→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    71→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name_when_supported_by_history"\n'
    72→        "  }],\n"
    73→        '  "retrospective": {\n'
    74→        '    "root_causes": ["optional: concise root-cause hypotheses"],\n'
    75→        '    "likely_symptoms": ["optional: identifiers that look symptom-level"],\n'
    76→        '    "possible_false_positives": ["optional: prior concept keys likely mis-scoped"]\n'
    77→        "  }\n"
    78→        "}\n"
    79→    )
    80→
    81→
    82→def render_batch_prompt(
    83→    *,
    84→    repo_root: Path,
    85→    packet_path: Path,
    86→    batch_index: int,
    87→    batch: dict[str, object],
    88→) -> str:
    89→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
    90→    context = build_batch_context(batch, batch_index)
    91→    return join_non_empty_sections(
    92→        _render_metadata_block(
    93→            repo_root=repo_root,
    94→            packet_path=packet_path,
    95→            batch_index=batch_index,
    96→            context=context,
    97→        ),
    98→        render_scoring_frame(),
    99→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   100→        render_seed_files_block(context),
   101→        render_historical_focus(batch),
   102→        render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   103→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=context.issues_cap, dim_set=context.dimension_set),
   104→        render_scope_enums(),
   105→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   106→    )
   107→
   108→
   109→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]
   110→
```

```
1→"""Shared prompt rendering sections used by both batch and external review paths."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
     8→    DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY,
     9→    HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD,
    10→    LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD,
    11→    max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class PromptBatchContext:
    17→    name: str
    18→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...]
    19→    rationale: str
    20→    seed_files: tuple[str, ...]
    21→    issues_cap: int
    22→
    23→    @property
    24→    def dimension_set(self) -> set[str]:
    25→        return set(self.dimensions)
    26→
    27→    @property
    28→    def dimensions_text(self) -> str:
    29→        return ", ".join(self.dimensions) if self.dimensions else "(none)"
    30→
    31→    @property
    32→    def seed_files_text(self) -> str:
    33→        return "\n".join(f"- {path}" for path in self.seed_files) if self.seed_files else "- (none)"
    34→
    35→
    36→def coerce_string_list(raw: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    37→    if not isinstance(raw, list | tuple):
    38→        return ()
    39→    return tuple(str(item) for item in raw if isinstance(item, str) and item)
    40→
    41→
    42→def build_batch_context(batch: dict[str, object], batch_index: int) -> PromptBatchContext:
    43→    dimensions = coerce_string_list(batch.get("dimensions", []))
    44→    return PromptBatchContext(
    45→        name=str(batch.get("name", f"Batch {batch_index + 1}")),
    46→        dimensions=dimensions,
    47→        rationale=str(batch.get("why", "")).strip(),
    48→        seed_files=coerce_string_list(batch.get("files_to_read", [])),
    49→        issues_cap=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dimensions)),
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION = {
    54→    "initialization_coupling": (
    55→        "9e. For initialization_coupling, use evidence from "
    56→        "`holistic_context.scan_evidence.mutable_globals` and "
    57→        "`holistic_context.errors.mutable_globals`. Investigate initialization ordering "
    58→        "dependencies, coupling through shared mutable state, and whether state should "
    59→        "be encapsulated behind a proper registry/context manager.\n"
    60→    ),
    61→    "design_coherence": (
    62→        "9f. For design_coherence, use evidence from "
    63→        "`holistic_context.scan_evidence.signal_density` — files where "
    64→        "multiple mechanical detectors fired. Investigate what design change would address "
    65→        "multiple signals simultaneously. Check `scan_evidence.complexity_hotspots` for "
    66→        "files with high responsibility cluster counts.\n"
    67→    ),
    68→    "error_consistency": (
    69→        "9g. For error_consistency, use evidence from "
    70→        "`holistic_context.errors.exception_hotspots` — files with "
    71→        "concentrated exception handling issues. Investigate whether error handling is "
    72→        "designed or accidental. Check for broad catches masking specific failure modes.\n"
    73→    ),
    74→    "cross_module_architecture": (
    75→        "9h. For cross_module_architecture, also consult "
    76→        "`holistic_context.coupling.boundary_violations` for import paths that "
    77→        "cross architectural boundaries, and `holistic_context.dependencies.deferred_import_density` "
    78→        "for files with many function-level imports (proxy for cycle pressure).\n"
    79→    ),
    80→    "convention_outlier": (
    81→        "9i. For convention_outlier, also consult "
    82→        "`holistic_context.conventions.duplicate_clusters` for cross-file "
    83→        "function duplication and `conventions.naming_drift` for directory-level naming "
    84→        "inconsistency.\n"
    85→    ),
    86→}
    87→
    88→
    89→def render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
    90→    """Render dimension-specific scan_evidence guidance."""
    91→    return "".join(
    92→        text
    93→        for dim, text in SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION.items()
    94→        if dim in dim_set
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def render_historical_focus(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
    99→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
   100→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
   101→        return ""
   102→
   103→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
   104→    try:
   105→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
   106→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   107→        selected_count = 0
   108→
   109→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])
   110→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   111→        issues = []
   112→
   113→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
   114→        return ""
   115→
   116→    lines: list[str] = []
   117→    lines.append(
   118→        "Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:"
   119→    )
   120→    lines.append(
   121→        "Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report"
   122→        " issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or"
   123→        " what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out."
   124→    )
   125→
   126→    for entry in issues:
   127→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   128→            continue
   129→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   130→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   131→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
   132→
   133→        line = f"  - [{status}] {summary}"
   134→        if note:
   135→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   136→        lines.append(line)
   137→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   138→
   139→
   140→def render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
   141→    """Render mechanically-generated concern hypotheses for this batch."""
   142→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")
   143→    if not isinstance(signals, list) or not signals:
   144→        return ""
   145→
   146→    lines: list[str] = []
   147→    lines.append("Mechanical concern signals — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:")
   148→    lines.append(
   149→        "Confirm or refute each with your own code reading. Report only confirmed defects."
   150→    )
   151→
   152→    shown = 0
   153→    for entry in signals:
   154→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   155→            continue
   156→        file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown file)"
   157→        concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   158→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   159→        question = str(entry.get("question", "")).strip()
   160→        evidence_raw = entry.get("evidence", [])
   161→        evidence = (
   162→            [str(item).strip() for item in evidence_raw if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
   163→            if isinstance(evidence_raw, list)
   164→            else []
   165→        )
   166→
   167→        lines.append(f"  - [{concern_type}] {file}")
   168→        if summary:
   169→            lines.append(f"    summary: {summary}")
   170→        if question:
   171→            lines.append(f"    question: {question}")
   172→        for snippet in evidence[:2]:
   173→            lines.append(f"    evidence: {snippet}")
   174→        shown += 1
   175→        if shown >= 8:
   176→            break
   177→
   178→    extra = max(0, len(signals) - shown)
   179→    if extra:
   180→        lines.append(f"  - (+{extra} more concern signals)")
   181→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   182→
   183→
   184→def render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   185→    """Render workflow integrity checks for architecture/integration dimensions."""
   186→    if not dim_set.intersection(
   187→        {
   188→            "cross_module_architecture",
   189→            "high_level_elegance",
   190→            "mid_level_elegance",
   191→            "design_coherence",
   192→            "initialization_coupling",
   193→        }
   194→    ):
   195→        return ""
   196→    return (
   197→        "9j. Workflow integrity checks: when reviewing orchestration/queue/review flows,\n"
   198→        "    explicitly look for loop-prone patterns and blind spots:\n"
   199→        "    - repeated stale/reopen churn without clear exit criteria or gating,\n"
   200→        "    - packet/batch data being generated but dropped before prompt execution,\n"
   201→        "    - ranking/triage logic that can starve target-improving work,\n"
   202→        "    - reruns happening before existing open review work is drained.\n"
   203→        "    If found, propose concrete guardrails and where to implement them.\n"
   204→    )
   205→
   206→
   207→def render_package_org_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   208→    if "package_organization" not in dim_set:
   209→        return ""
   210→    return (
   211→        "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   212→        "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   213→        "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   214→        "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   215→        "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   216→        "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   217→        "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_issues` for directories "
   218→        "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   219→    )
   220→
   221→
   222→def render_abstraction_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   223→    if "abstraction_fitness" not in dim_set:
   224→        return ""
   225→    return (
   226→        "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   227→        "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   228→        "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   229→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   230→        "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   231→        "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   232→        "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   233→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   234→        "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   235→        "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   236→        "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   237→    )
   238→
   239→
   240→def render_dimension_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   241→    return (
   242→        render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   243→        + render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   244→        + render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   245→        + render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   246→    )
   247→
   248→
   249→def render_scoring_frame() -> str:
   250→    return (
   251→        "YOUR TASK: Read the code for this batch's dimensions. For each dimension, judge "
   252→        "how well the codebase serves a developer from that perspective. The dimension "
   253→        "prompt in the blind packet defines what good looks like — that is your rubric. "
   254→        "Cite specific observations that explain your judgment.\n\n"
   255→        "WHAT WE GIVE YOU AND WHY: Below you will find automated scan evidence, historical "
   256→        "issues, and mechanical concern signals. These are navigation aids — starting points "
   257→        "for where to look. They are NOT evidence, NOT findings, and NOT inputs to your "
   258→        "scores. Only what you observe directly in the code informs your judgment.\n\n"
   259→    )
   260→
   261→
   262→def render_scan_evidence_note() -> str:
   263→    return (
   264→        "Mechanical scan evidence — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\n"
   265→        "The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` with aggregated signals "
   266→        "from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, error hotspots, signal "
   267→        "density index, boundary violations, and systemic patterns. Use these as starting "
   268→        "points for where to look beyond the seed files.\n\n"
   269→    )
   270→
   271→
   272→def render_seed_files_block(context: PromptBatchContext) -> str:
   273→    return f"Seed files (start here):\n{context.seed_files_text}\n\n"
   274→
   275→
   276→def render_task_requirements(*, issues_cap: int, dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   277→    return (
   278→        "Task requirements:\n"
   279→        "1. Read the blind packet and follow `system_prompt` constraints exactly.\n"
   280→        "1a. If previously flagged issues are listed above, use them as context for your review.\n"
   281→        "    Verify whether each still applies to the current code. Do not re-report fixed or\n"
   282→        "    wontfix issues. Use them as starting points to look deeper — inspect adjacent code\n"
   283→        "    and related modules for defects the prior review may have missed.\n"
   284→        "1b. If mechanical concern signals are listed above, confirm or refute each with your\n"
   285→        "    own code reading. Report only confirmed defects.\n"
   286→        "1c. Think structurally: when you spot multiple individual issues that share a common\n"
   287→        "    root cause (missing abstraction, duplicated pattern, inconsistent convention),\n"
   288→        "    explain the deeper structural issue in the issue, not just the surface symptom.\n"
   289→        "    If the pattern is significant enough, report the structural issue as its own issue\n"
   290→        "    with appropriate fix_scope ('multi_file_refactor' or 'architectural_change') and\n"
   291→        "    use `root_cause_cluster` to connect related symptom issues together.\n"
   292→        "2. Start with the seed files, then freely explore additional repository files likely to surface material issues.\n"
   293→        "2a. Prioritize high-signal leads: unexplored/lightly reviewed files, historical issue areas, and hotspot neighbors "
   294→        "(high coupling, god modules, large files, churn seams).\n"
   295→        "2b. Keep exploration targeted — follow strongest evidence paths first instead of attempting exhaustive coverage.\n"
   296→        "2c. Keep issues and scoring scoped to this batch's listed dimensions.\n"
   297→        "2d. Respect scope controls in the blind packet config: do not include files/directories marked by "
   298→        "`exclude`, `suppress`, or zone overrides that classify files as non-production (test/config/generated/vendor).\n"
   299→        f"3. Return 0-{issues_cap} high-quality issues for this batch (empty array allowed).\n"
   300→        "3a. Do not suppress real defects to keep scores high; report every material issue you can support with evidence.\n"
   301→        "3b. Do not default to 100. Reserve 100 for genuinely exemplary evidence in this batch.\n"
   302→        "4. Score/issue consistency is required: broader or more severe issues MUST lower dimension scores.\n"
   303→        f"4a. Any dimension scored below {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include explicit feedback: add at least one "
   304→        "issue with the same `dimension` and a non-empty actionable `suggestion`.\n"
   305→        "5. Every issue must include `related_files` with at least 2 files when possible.\n"
   306→        "6. Every issue must include `dimension`, `identifier`, `summary`, `evidence`, `suggestion`, and `confidence`.\n"
   307→        "7. Every issue must include `impact_scope` and `fix_scope`.\n"
   308→        "8. Every scored dimension MUST include dimension_notes with concrete evidence.\n"
   309→        f"9. If a dimension score is >{HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f}, include `{DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY}` in dimension_notes.\n"
   310→        "10. Use exactly one decimal place for every assessment and abstraction sub-axis score.\n"
   311→        f"{render_dimension_focus(dim_set)}"
   312→        "11. Ignore prior chat context and any target-threshold assumptions.\n"
   313→        "12. Do not edit repository files.\n"
   314→        "13. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.\n\n"
   315→    )
   316→
   317→
   318→def render_scope_enums() -> str:
   319→    return (
   320→        "Scope enums:\n"
   321→        '- impact_scope: "local" | "module" | "subsystem" | "codebase"\n'
   322→        '- fix_scope: "single_edit" | "multi_file_refactor" | "architectural_change"\n\n'
   323→    )
   324→
   325→
   326→def join_non_empty_sections(*sections: str) -> str:
   327→    return "".join(section for section in sections if section)
   328→
   329→
```

```
    59→def _utc_now() -> datetime:

    63→def _iso_seconds(dt: datetime) -> str:
    64→    return dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")

    67→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    68→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    69→        return None
    70→    try:
    71→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    72→    except ValueError:
    73→        return None
    74→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    75→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    76→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)

    91→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    92→    if not session_id.strip():

    94→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}

    99→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   100→    if not path.exists():
   101→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
   102→    try:
   103→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   104→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   105→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
   106→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   107→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
   108→    return payload

   111→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   112→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   113→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   114→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   115→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   116→    if payload_id != session_id:
   117→        raise CommandError(
   118→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   119→        )
   120→    return path, payload

   248→    output_schema = (

   297→    """Start an external review session with CLI-issued provenance context."""

   418→def _canonical_external_payload(
   419→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   420→    *,
   421→    session: dict[str, Any],
   422→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   423→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   424→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   425→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   426→        raise CommandError(
   427→            "Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object."
   428→            ' Expected: {"session":{"id":"...","token":"..."},"assessments":{...},"issues":[...]}',
   429→        )
   430→
   431→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   432→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   433→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   434→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   435→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   436→        raise CommandError(
   437→            "Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload."
   438→            " Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.",
   439→        )
   440→
   441→    payload = {
   442→        key: value
   443→        for key, value in raw_payload.items()
   444→        if key not in {"session", "provenance"}
   445→    }
   446→    payload["provenance"] = {
   447→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   448→        "blind": True,
   449→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   450→        "session_id": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   451→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   452→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   453→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   454→    }
   455→    return payload
   456→
   457→
   458→def _ensure_session_open(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   459→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   460→    if status == "open":
   461→        return
   462→    raise CommandError(
   463→        f"Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   464→    )
   465→
   466→
   467→def _ensure_session_not_expired(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   468→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   469→    if expires_at is None:
   470→        raise CommandError(
   471→            "Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   472→        )
   473→    now = _utc_now()
   474→    if now <= expires_at:
   475→        return
   476→    raise CommandError(
   477→        f"Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   478→    )
   479→
   480→
   481→def do_external_submit(
   482→    *,
   483→    import_file: str,
   484→    session_id: str,
   485→    state: dict,
   486→    lang,
   487→    state_file,
   488→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   489→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   490→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   491→    scan_path: str = ".",
   492→    dry_run: bool = False,
   493→) -> None:
   494→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   495→    config = config or {}
   496→    session_path, session = _session_payload(session_id)
   497→    _ensure_session_open(session)
   498→    _ensure_session_not_expired(session)
   499→
   500→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   501→        raise CommandError(
   502→            "Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   503→        )
   504→
   505→    issues_path = Path(import_file)
   506→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(issues_path, label="external issues")
   507→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   508→
   509→    stamp = run_stamp()
   510→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   511→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   512→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   513→
   514→    if dry_run:
   515→        do_validate_import(
   516→            str(canonical_path),
   517→            lang,
   518→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   519→            attested_external=True,
   520→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   521→        )
   522→        return
   523→
   524→    do_import(
   525→        str(canonical_path),
   526→        state,
   527→        lang,
   528→        state_file,
   529→        config=config,
   530→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   531→        attested_external=True,
   532→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   533→    )
   534→
   535→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   536→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   537→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   538→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(issues_path)
   539→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   540→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   541→
   542→    if scan_after_import:
   543→        code = run_followup_scan(
   544→            lang_name=lang.name,
   545→            scan_path=scan_path,
   546→            deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   547→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   548→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   549→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   550→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   551→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   552→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   553→            ),
   554→        )
   555→        if code != 0:
   556→            raise CommandError(f"External review exited with code {code}", exit_code=code)
   557→
   558→
   559→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   560→
```

```
   111→def parse_batch_selection(raw: str | None, batch_count: int) -> list[int]:
   112→    """Parse optional 1-based CSV list of batches."""
   113→    if not raw:
   114→        return list(range(batch_count))
   115→
   116→    selected: list[int] = []
   117→    seen: set[int] = set()
   118→    for token in raw.split(","):
   119→        text = token.strip()
   120→        if not text:
   121→            continue
   122→        idx_1 = int(text)
   123→        if idx_1 < 1 or idx_1 > batch_count:
   124→            raise ValueError(f"batch index {idx_1} out of range 1..{batch_count}")
   125→        idx_0 = idx_1 - 1
   126→        if idx_0 in seen:
   127→            continue
   128→        seen.add(idx_0)
   129→        selected.append(idx_0)
   130→    return selected
   131→
   132→
   133→def extract_json_payload(raw: str, *, log_fn) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   134→    """Best-effort extraction of first JSON object from agent output text."""
   135→    text = raw.strip()
   136→    if not text:
   137→        return None
   138→
   139→    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
   140→    last_decode_error: json.JSONDecodeError | None = None
   141→    for start, ch in enumerate(text):
   142→        if ch not in "{[":
   143→            continue
   144→        try:
   145→            obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[start:])
   146→        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   147→            last_decode_error = exc
   148→            continue
   149→        if (
   150→            isinstance(obj, dict)
   151→            and isinstance(obj.get("assessments"), dict)
   152→            and isinstance(obj.get("issues"), list)
   153→        ):
   154→            return obj
   155→    if last_decode_error is not None:
   156→        log_fn(f"  batch output JSON parse failed: {last_decode_error.msg}")
   157→    else:
   158→        log_fn("  batch output JSON parse failed: no valid payload found")
   159→    return None
   160→
   161→
   162→def _validate_dimension_note(
   163→    key: str,
   164→    note_raw: object,
   165→) -> tuple[list[object], str, str, str, str]:
   166→    """Validate a single dimension_notes entry and return parsed fields.
   167→
   168→    Returns (evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_preventing_higher_score).
   169→    Raises ValueError on invalid structure.
   170→    """
   171→    if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   172→        raise ValueError(
   173→            f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   174→        )
   175→    evidence = note_raw.get("evidence")
   176→    impact_scope = note_raw.get("impact_scope")
   177→    fix_scope = note_raw.get("fix_scope")
   178→    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or not evidence:
   179→        raise ValueError(
   180→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.evidence must be a non-empty array"
   181→        )
   182→    if not isinstance(impact_scope, str) or not impact_scope.strip():
   183→        raise ValueError(
   184→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.impact_scope must be a non-empty string"
   185→        )
   186→    if not isinstance(fix_scope, str) or not fix_scope.strip():
   187→        raise ValueError(
   188→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.fix_scope must be a non-empty string"
   189→        )
   190→
   191→    confidence_raw = str(note_raw.get("confidence", "medium")).strip().lower()
   192→    confidence = (
   193→        confidence_raw if confidence_raw in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "medium"
   194→    )
   195→    issues_note = str(note_raw.get(DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   196→    if not issues_note:
   197→        issues_note = str(note_raw.get(LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   198→    return evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note
   199→
   200→
   201→def _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   202→    note_raw: dict[str, object],
   203→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   204→) -> dict[str, float]:
   205→    """Extract and clamp abstraction_fitness sub-axis scores from a note."""
   206→    sub_axes_raw = note_raw.get("sub_axes")
   207→    if sub_axes_raw is not None and not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
   208→        raise ValueError(
   209→            "dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes must be an object"
   210→        )
   211→    if not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
   212→        return {}
   213→
   214→    normalized: dict[str, float] = {}
   215→    for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   216→        axis_value = sub_axes_raw.get(axis)
   217→        if axis_value is None:
   218→            continue
   219→        if isinstance(axis_value, bool) or not isinstance(
   220→            axis_value, int | float
   221→        ):
   222→            raise ValueError(
   223→                f"dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes.{axis} "
   224→                "must be numeric"
   225→            )
   226→        normalized[axis] = round(
   227→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(axis_value))),
   228→            1,
   229→        )
   230→    return normalized
   231→
   232→
   233→def _normalize_issues(
   234→    raw_issues: object,
   235→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   236→    *,
   237→    max_batch_issues: int,
   238→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   239→    low_score_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   240→) -> list[NormalizedBatchIssue]:
   241→    """Validate and normalize the issues array from a batch payload."""
   242→    if not isinstance(raw_issues, list):
   243→        raise ValueError("issues must be an array")
   244→
   245→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   246→    errors: list[str] = []
   247→    for idx, item in enumerate(raw_issues):
   248→        issue: ReviewIssuePayload | None
   249→        issue, issue_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
   250→            item,
   251→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
   252→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dims,
   253→            allow_dismissed=False,
   254→        )
   255→        if issue_errors:
   256→            errors.extend(issue_errors)
   257→            continue
   258→        if issue is None:
   259→            raise ValueError(
   260→                "batch issue payload missing after validation succeeded"
   261→            )
   262→
   263→        dim = issue["dimension"]
   264→        note = dimension_notes.get(dim, {})
   265→        impact_scope = str(
   266→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   267→                "impact_scope", note.get("impact_scope", "")
   268→            )
   269→        ).strip()
   270→        fix_scope = str(
   271→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   272→                "fix_scope", note.get("fix_scope", "")
   273→            )
   274→        ).strip()
   275→        if not impact_scope or not fix_scope:
   276→            errors.append(
   277→                f"issues[{idx}] requires impact_scope and fix_scope "
   278→                "(or dimension_notes defaults)"
   279→            )
   280→            continue
   281→        issues.append(
   282→            NormalizedBatchIssue(
   283→                dimension=issue["dimension"],
   284→                identifier=issue["identifier"],
   285→                summary=issue["summary"],
   286→                confidence=issue["confidence"],
   287→                suggestion=issue["suggestion"],
   288→                related_files=list(issue.get("related_files", [])),
   289→                evidence=list(issue.get("evidence", [])),
   290→                impact_scope=impact_scope,
   291→                fix_scope=fix_scope,
   292→                reasoning=str(issue.get("reasoning", "")),
   293→                evidence_lines=list(issue.get("evidence_lines", []))
   294→                if isinstance(issue.get("evidence_lines"), list)
   295→                else None,
   296→            )
   297→        )
   298→    if errors:
   299→        visible = errors[:10]
   300→        remaining = len(errors) - len(visible)
   301→        if remaining > 0:
   302→            visible.append(f"... {remaining} additional issue schema error(s) omitted")
   303→        raise ValueError("; ".join(visible))
   304→    if len(issues) <= max_batch_issues:
   305→        return issues
   306→
   307→    required_dims = set(low_score_dimensions or set())
   308→    if not required_dims:
   309→        return issues[:max_batch_issues]
   310→
   311→    # Preserve at least one issue per low-score dimension before trimming.
   312→    selected: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   313→    selected_indexes: set[int] = set()
   314→    covered: set[str] = set()
   315→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   316→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   317→            break
   318→        dim = issue.dimension.strip()
   319→        if dim not in required_dims or dim in covered:
   320→            continue
   321→        selected.append(issue)
   322→        selected_indexes.add(idx)
   323→        covered.add(dim)
   324→
   325→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   326→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   327→            break
   328→        if idx in selected_indexes:
   329→            continue
   330→        selected.append(issue)
   331→    return selected
   332→
   333→
   334→def _low_score_dimensions(assessments: dict[str, float]) -> set[str]:
   335→    """Return assessed dimensions requiring explicit defect issues."""
   336→    return {
   337→        dim
   338→        for dim, score in assessments.items()
   339→        if score < LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD
   340→    }
   341→
   342→
   343→def _enforce_low_score_issues(
   344→    *,
   345→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   346→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   347→) -> None:
   348→    """Fail closed when low scores do not report explicit issues."""
   349→    required_dims = _low_score_dimensions(assessments)
   350→    if not required_dims:
   351→        return
   352→    issue_dims = {
   353→        issue.dimension.strip() for issue in issues
   354→    }
   355→    missing = sorted(dim for dim in required_dims if dim not in issue_dims)
   356→    if not missing:
   357→        return
   358→    joined = ", ".join(missing)
   359→    raise ValueError(
   360→        "low-score dimensions must include at least one explicit issue: "
   361→        f"{joined} (threshold {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f})"
   362→    )
   363→
   364→
   365→def _compute_batch_quality(
   366→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   367→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   368→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   369→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   370→    high_score_missing_issue_note: float,
   371→) -> BatchQualityPayload:
   372→    """Compute quality metrics for a single batch result."""
   373→    return {
   374→        "dimension_coverage": round(
   375→            len(assessments) / max(len(allowed_dims), 1),
   376→            3,
   377→        ),
   378→        "evidence_density": round(
   379→            sum(len(note.get("evidence", [])) for note in dimension_notes.values())
   380→            / max(len(issues), 1),
   381→            3,
   382→        ),
   383→        "high_score_missing_issue_note": high_score_missing_issue_note,
   384→    }
   385→
   386→
   387→def normalize_batch_result(
   388→    payload: dict[str, object],
   389→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   390→    *,
   391→    max_batch_issues: int,
   392→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   393→) -> tuple[
   394→    dict[str, float],
   395→    list[BatchIssuePayload],
   396→    dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   397→    BatchQualityPayload,
   398→]:
   399→    """Validate and normalize one batch payload."""
   400→    if "assessments" not in payload:
   401→        raise ValueError("payload missing required key: assessments")
   402→    key_error = normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
   403→        payload,
   404→        missing_issues_error="payload missing required key: issues",
   405→    )
   406→    if key_error is not None:
   407→        raise ValueError(key_error)
   408→
   409→    raw_assessments = payload.get("assessments")
   410→    if not isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):
   411→        raise ValueError("assessments must be an object")
   412→
   413→    raw_dimension_notes = payload.get("dimension_notes", {})
   414→    if not isinstance(raw_dimension_notes, dict):
   415→        raise ValueError("dimension_notes must be an object")
   416→
   417→    assessments: dict[str, float] = {}
   418→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload] = {}
   419→    high_score_missing_issue_note = 0.0
   420→    for key, value in raw_assessments.items():
   421→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key:
   422→            continue
   423→        if key not in allowed_dims:
   424→            continue
   425→        if isinstance(value, bool):
   426→            continue
   427→        if not isinstance(value, int | float):
   428→            continue
   429→        score = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
   430→
   431→        note_raw = raw_dimension_notes.get(key)
   432→        evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note = (
   433→            _validate_dimension_note(key, note_raw)
   434→        )
   435→        if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   436→            raise ValueError(
   437→                f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   438→            )
   439→        if score > HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD and not issues_note:
   440→            high_score_missing_issue_note += 1
   441→
   442→        normalized_sub_axes: dict[str, float] = {}
   443→        if key == "abstraction_fitness":
   444→            normalized_sub_axes = _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   445→                note_raw, abstraction_sub_axes
   446→            )
   447→
   448→        assessments[key] = score
   449→        dimension_notes[key] = {
   450→            "evidence": [str(item).strip() for item in evidence if str(item).strip()],
   451→            "impact_scope": impact_scope.strip(),
   452→            "fix_scope": fix_scope.strip(),
   453→            "confidence": confidence,
   454→            "issues_preventing_higher_score": issues_note,
   455→        }
   456→        if normalized_sub_axes:
   457→            dimension_notes[key]["sub_axes"] = normalized_sub_axes
   458→
   459→    issues = _normalize_issues(
   460→        payload.get("issues"),
   461→        dimension_notes,
   462→        max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues,
   463→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   464→        low_score_dimensions=_low_score_dimensions(assessments),
   465→    )
   466→    _enforce_low_score_issues(assessments=assessments, issues=issues)
   467→
   468→    quality = _compute_batch_quality(
   469→        assessments,
   470→        issues,
   471→        dimension_notes,
   472→        allowed_dims,
   473→        high_score_missing_issue_note,
   474→    )
   475→    return (
   476→        assessments,
   477→        [issue.to_payload() for issue in issues],
   478→        dimension_notes,
   479→        quality,
   480→    )
   481→
   482→
   483→def assessment_weight(
   484→    *,
   485→    dimension: str,
   486→    issues: list[BatchIssuePayload],
   487→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   488→) -> float:
   489→    """Evidence-weighted assessment score weight with a neutral floor.
   490→
   491→    Weighting is evidence-based and score-independent: the raw score does not
   492→    influence how much weight a batch contributes during merge.
   493→    """
   494→    note = dimension_notes.get(dimension, {})
   495→    note_evidence = len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else 0
   496→    issue_count = sum(
   497→        1
   498→        for issue in issues
   499→        if str(issue.get("dimension", "")).strip() == dimension
   500→    )
   501→    return float(1 + note_evidence + issue_count)
   502→
   503→
   504→def _issue_pressure_by_dimension(
   505→    issues: list[BatchIssuePayload],
   506→    *,
   507→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   508→) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, int]]:
   509→    """Summarize how strongly issues should pull dimension scores down."""
   510→    return _DIMENSION_SCORER.issue_pressure_by_dimension(
   511→        issues,
   512→        dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   513→    )
   514→
   515→
   516→def _accumulate_batch_scores(
   517→    result: BatchResultPayload,
   518→    *,
   519→    score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   520→    score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]],
   521→    merged_dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   522→    abstraction_axis_scores: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   523→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   524→) -> None:
   525→    """Accumulate assessment scores, dimension notes, and sub-axis data from one batch."""
   526→    result_issues = result.get("issues", [])
   527→    result_notes = result.get("dimension_notes", {})
   528→    for key, score in result.get("assessments", {}).items():
   529→        if isinstance(score, bool):
   530→            continue
   531→        score_value = float(score)
   532→        weight = assessment_weight(
   533→            dimension=key,
   534→            issues=result_issues,
   535→            dimension_notes=result_notes,
   536→        )
   537→        score_buckets.setdefault(key, []).append((score_value, weight))
   538→        score_raw_by_dim.setdefault(key, []).append(score_value)
   539→
   540→        note = result_notes.get(key)
   541→        existing = merged_dimension_notes.get(key)
   542→        existing_evidence = (
   543→            len(existing.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(existing, dict) else -1
   544→        )
   545→        current_evidence = (
   546→            len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else -1
   547→        )
   548→        if current_evidence > existing_evidence and note is not None:
   549→            merged_dimension_notes[key] = note
   550→
   551→        if key == "abstraction_fitness" and isinstance(note, dict):
   552→            sub_axes = note.get("sub_axes")
   553→            if isinstance(sub_axes, dict):
   554→                for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   555→                    axis_score = sub_axes.get(axis)
   556→                    if isinstance(axis_score, bool) or not isinstance(
   557→                        axis_score, int | float
   558→                    ):
   559→                        continue
   560→                    abstraction_axis_scores[axis].append(
   561→                        (float(axis_score), weight)
   562→                    )
   563→
   564→
   565→def _issue_identity_key(issue: BatchIssuePayload) -> str:
   566→    """Build a stable concept key; prefer dimension+identifier when available."""
   567→    dim = str(issue.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   568→    ident = str(issue.get("identifier", "")).strip()
   569→    if ident:
   570→        return f"{dim}::{ident}"
   571→    summary = str(issue.get("summary", "")).strip()
   572→    summary_terms = sorted(normalize_word_set(summary))
   573→    if summary_terms:
   574→        return f"{dim}::summary::{','.join(summary_terms[:8])}"
   575→    return f"{dim}::{summary}"
   576→
   577→
   578→def _merge_issue_payload(
   579→    existing: BatchIssuePayload, incoming: BatchIssuePayload
   580→) -> None:
   581→    """Merge two concept-equivalent issues into the existing payload."""
   582→    merge_list_fields(existing, incoming, ("related_files", "evidence"))
   583→    # Prefer richer summary/suggestion text when they differ.
   584→    pick_longer_text(existing, incoming, "summary")
   585→    pick_longer_text(existing, incoming, "suggestion")
   586→    track_merged_from(existing, str(incoming.get("identifier", "")).strip())
   587→
   588→
   589→def _should_merge_issues(
   590→    existing: BatchIssuePayload, incoming: BatchIssuePayload
   591→) -> bool:
   592→    """Check whether two key-matched issues are similar enough to merge."""
   593→    existing_summary = normalize_word_set(str(existing.get("summary", "")))
   594→    incoming_summary = normalize_word_set(str(incoming.get("summary", "")))
   595→    if existing_summary and incoming_summary:
   596→        overlap = len(existing_summary & incoming_summary)
   597→        union = len(existing_summary | incoming_summary)
   598→        if union and overlap / union >= 0.3:
   599→            return True
   600→    # Fall back to related-file overlap
   601→    existing_files = set(cast(list[str], existing.get("related_files", [])))
   602→    incoming_files = set(cast(list[str], incoming.get("related_files", [])))
   603→    if existing_files and incoming_files:
   604→        return bool(existing_files & incoming_files)
   605→    # When no corroborating signal is available, allow merge
   606→    return not existing_summary or not incoming_summary
   607→
   608→
   609→def _accumulate_batch_quality(
   610→    result: BatchResultPayload,
   611→    *,
   612→    coverage_values: list[float],
   613→    evidence_density_values: list[float],
   614→) -> float:
   615→    """Accumulate quality metrics from one batch. Returns high-score-missing-issues delta."""
   616→    quality: object = result.get("quality", {})
   617→    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
   618→        return 0.0
   619→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")
   620→    density = quality.get("evidence_density")
   621→    missing_issue_note = quality.get(REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY)
   622→    if not isinstance(missing_issue_note, int | float):
   623→        missing_issue_note = quality.get(
   624→            LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
   625→        )
   626→    if isinstance(coverage, int | float):
   627→        coverage_values.append(float(coverage))
   628→    if isinstance(density, int | float):
   629→        evidence_density_values.append(float(density))
   630→    return (
   631→        float(missing_issue_note)
   632→        if isinstance(missing_issue_note, int | float)
   633→        else 0.0
   634→    )
   635→
   636→
   637→def _compute_merged_assessments(
   638→    score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   639→    score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]],
   640→    issue_pressure_by_dim: dict[str, float],
   641→    issue_count_by_dim: dict[str, int],
   642→) -> dict[str, float]:
   643→    """Compute pressure-adjusted weighted mean for each dimension."""
   644→    return _DIMENSION_SCORER.merge_scores(
   645→        score_buckets,
   646→        score_raw_by_dim,
   647→        issue_pressure_by_dim,
   648→        issue_count_by_dim,
   649→    )
   650→
   651→
   652→def _compute_abstraction_components(
   653→    merged_assessments: dict[str, float],
   654→    abstraction_axis_scores: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   655→    *,
   656→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   657→    abstraction_component_names: dict[str, str],
   658→) -> dict[str, float] | None:
   659→    """Compute weighted abstraction sub-axis component scores.
   660→
   661→    Returns component_scores dict, or None if abstraction_fitness is not assessed.
   662→    """
   663→    abstraction_score = merged_assessments.get("abstraction_fitness")
   664→    if abstraction_score is None:
   665→        return None
   666→
   667→    component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   668→    for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   669→        weighted = abstraction_axis_scores.get(axis, [])
   670→        if not weighted:
   671→            continue
   672→        numerator = sum(score * weight for score, weight in weighted)
   673→        denominator = sum(weight for _, weight in weighted)
   674→        if denominator <= 0:
   675→            continue
   676→        component_scores[abstraction_component_names[axis]] = round(
   677→            max(0.0, min(100.0, numerator / denominator)),
   678→            1,
   679→        )
   680→    return component_scores if component_scores else None
   681→
   682→
   683→def merge_batch_results(
   684→    batch_results: list[BatchResultPayload],
   685→    *,
   686→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   687→    abstraction_component_names: dict[str, str],
   688→) -> dict[str, object]:
   689→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
   690→    from .merge import merge_batch_results as _merge_batch_results
   691→
   692→    return _merge_batch_results(
   693→        batch_results,
   694→        abstraction_sub_axes=abstraction_sub_axes,
   695→        abstraction_component_names=abstraction_component_names,
   696→    )
   697→
   698→
   699→def build_batch_prompt(
   700→    *,
   701→    repo_root: Path,
   702→    packet_path: Path,
   703→    batch_index: int,
   704→    batch: dict[str, object],
   705→) -> str:
   706→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   707→    return render_batch_prompt(
   708→        repo_root=repo_root,
   709→        packet_path=packet_path,
   710→        batch_index=batch_index,
   711→        batch=batch,
   712→    )
   713→
   714→
   715→__all__ = [
   716→    "assessment_weight",
   717→    "build_batch_prompt",
   718→    "extract_json_payload",
   719→    "merge_batch_results",
   720→    "normalize_batch_result",
   721→    "parse_batch_selection",
   722→]
   723→
```

```
   284→    )
   285→
   286→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   287→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   288→
   289→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   290→    """
   291→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   292→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   293→
   294→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   295→    if not summary_path.exists():
   296→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   297→    try:
   298→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   299→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   300→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   301→
   302→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   303→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   304→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   305→
   306→    if not successful:
   307→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   308→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   309→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   310→
   311→    try:
   312→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   313→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   314→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   315→
   316→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   317→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   318→
   319→
   320→def do_import_run(
   321→    run_dir_path: str,
   322→    state: dict,
   323→    lang,
   324→    state_file: str,
   325→    *,
   326→    config: dict | None = None,
   327→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   328→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   329→    scan_path: str = ".",
   330→) -> None:
   331→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   332→
   333→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   334→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   335→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   336→    """
   337→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   338→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   339→
   340→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   341→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   342→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   343→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   344→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   345→
   346→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   347→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   348→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   349→    output_files = {
   350→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   351→        for idx in selected_indexes
   352→    }
   353→
   354→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   355→    if missing:
   356→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   357→
   358→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   359→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   360→        failures=[],
   361→        output_files=output_files,
   362→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   363→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   364→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   365→            payload,
   366→            dims,
   367→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   368→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   369→        ),
   370→    )
   371→
   372→    if not batch_results:
   373→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   374→
   375→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   376→    if failures:
   377→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   378→
   379→    # -- merge --
   380→    merged = _merge_batch_results(batch_results)
   381→
   382→    # -- build provenance --
   383→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   384→    merged["provenance"] = build_batch_import_provenance(
   385→        runner=runner,
   386→        blind_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   387→        run_stamp=stamp,
   388→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   389→    )
   390→
   391→    # -- write merged output --
   392→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   393→    safe_write_text(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   394→    print(colorize(f"  Merged output: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   395→
   396→    # -- import with trusted source --
   397→    _do_import(
   398→        str(merged_path),
   399→        state,
   400→        lang,
   401→        state_file,
   402→        config=config,
   403→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   404→        trusted_assessment_source=True,
   405→        trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   409→    if scan_after_import:
   410→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   411→        if lang_name:
   412→            run_followup_scan(
   413→                lang_name=lang_name,
   414→                scan_path=scan_path,
   415→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   416→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   417→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   418→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   419→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   420→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   421→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   422→                ),
   423→            )
   424→
```

```
   246→                ]
   247→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   248→                if newly_warned:
   249→                    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   250→                    warning_message = (

   261→            return

   375→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   376→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   377→        write_run_summary(
   378→            successful_batches=[],
   379→            failed_batches=[],
   380→            interrupted=True,
   381→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   382→        )
   383→        append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   384→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   385→
   386→    allowed_dims = {
   387→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   388→    }
   389→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   390→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   391→        failures=execution_failures,
   392→        output_files=output_files,
   393→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   397→    failure_set = set(failures)
   398→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   399→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   400→        key = str(idx + 1)
   401→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   402→            key,
   403→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   404→        )
   405→        if idx not in failure_set:
   406→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   407→            continue
   408→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   409→            state["status"] = "failed"
   410→            continue
   411→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   412→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   413→            continue
   414→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   415→
   416→    write_run_summary(
   417→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   418→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   419→    )
   420→
   421→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   422→        append_run_log(
   423→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   424→        )
   425→        print_failures_and_raise_fn(
   426→            failures=failures,
   427→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   428→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   429→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   430→        )
   431→    elif failures:
   432→        print(
   433→            colorize_fn(
   434→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   435→                "yellow",
   436→            )
   437→        )
   438→        print_failures_fn(
   439→            failures=failures,
   440→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   441→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   442→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   443→        )
   444→        append_run_log(
   445→            "run-partial "
   446→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   447→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   448→        )
   449→
   450→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   451→    reviewed_files = collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   452→        batches=batches,
   453→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   454→    )
   455→    full_sweep_included = any(
   456→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   457→        for idx in successful_indexes
   458→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   459→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   460→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   461→    )
   462→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   463→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   464→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   465→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   466→    }
   467→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   468→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   469→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   470→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   471→    if reviewed_files:
   472→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   473→        print(
   474→            colorize_fn(
   475→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   476→                "dim",
   477→            )
   478→        )
   479→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   480→        runner=runner,
   481→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   482→        run_stamp=stamp,
   483→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   484→    )
   485→    merged_assessment_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   486→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   487→    )
   488→    merged_issue_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   489→        [
   490→            issue.get("dimension")
   491→            for issue in (merged.get("issues") or [])

   493→        ]
   494→    )
   495→    merged_imported_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   496→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_issue_dims
   497→    )
   498→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   499→    missing_after_import = print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   500→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   501→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   502→        scan_path=scan_path,
   503→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   504→    )
   505→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {

   507→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   508→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,

   511→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"

   513→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   514→    print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)

   516→    try:
```

```
    61→@dataclass
    62→class ReviewPrepareOptions:
    63→    """Configuration bundle for per-file review preparation."""
    64→
    65→    max_files: int | None = None
    66→    max_age_days: int = 30
    67→    force_refresh: bool = True

    84→
    85→def _rel_list(s: set[str] | list[str]) -> list[str]:
    86→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    87→    if isinstance(s, set):
    88→        return sorted(rel(x) for x in s)[:10]
    89→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]
    90→
    91→
    92→def _normalize_max_files(value: Any) -> int | None:
    93→    """Normalize max_files input: None/<=0 means unlimited."""
    94→    if value in (None, ""):
    95→        return None
    96→    parsed = int(value)
    97→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
    98→
    99→
   100→def prepare_review(
   101→    path: Path,
   102→    lang: object,
   103→    state: dict,
   104→    options: ReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   105→) -> dict[str, object]:
   106→    """Prepare review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict.
   107→
   108→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks
   109→    when the caller already has the file list, e.g. from _setup_lang).
   110→    """
   111→    resolved_options = options or ReviewPrepareOptions()
   112→    resolved_options.max_files = _normalize_max_files(resolved_options.max_files)
   113→    all_files = (
   114→        resolved_options.files
   115→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   116→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   117→    )
   118→
   119→    # Enable file cache for entire prepare operation — context building,
   120→    # file selection, and content extraction all read the same files.
   121→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   122→    if not already_cached:
   123→        enable_file_cache()
   124→    try:
   125→        context = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   126→        selected = select_files_for_review(
   127→            lang,
   128→            path,
   129→            state,
   130→            options=ReviewSelectionOptions(
   131→                max_files=resolved_options.max_files,
   132→                max_age_days=resolved_options.max_age_days,
   133→                force_refresh=resolved_options.force_refresh,
   134→                files=all_files,
   135→            ),
   136→        )
   137→        file_requests = _build_file_requests(selected, lang, state)
   138→    finally:
   139→        if not already_cached:
   140→            disable_file_cache()
   141→
   142→    default_dims, dimension_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   143→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   144→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   145→        config_dimensions=resolved_options.config_dimensions,
   146→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   147→    )
   148→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   149→    valid_dims = set(dimension_prompts)
   150→    invalid_requested = [
   151→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   152→    ]
   153→    invalid_config = [
   154→        dim
   155→        for dim in (resolved_options.config_dimensions or [])
   156→        if dim not in valid_dims
   157→    ]
   158→
   159→    return {
   160→        "command": "review",
   161→        "language": lang.name,
   162→        "dimensions": dims,
   163→        "dimension_prompts": {
   164→            d: dimension_prompts[d] for d in dims if d in dimension_prompts
   165→        },
   166→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   167→        "context": serialize_context(context),
   168→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   169→        "files": file_requests,
   170→        "total_candidates": len(file_requests),
   171→        "cache_status": {
   172→            "fresh": count_fresh(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   173→            "stale": count_stale(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   174→            "new": len(file_requests),
   175→        },
   176→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   177→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   178→            "config": invalid_config,
   179→        },
   180→    }
   181→
   182→
   183→def _build_file_requests(files: list[str], lang: object, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   184→    """Build per-file review request dicts."""
   185→    file_requests = []
   186→    for filepath in files:
   187→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   188→        if content is None:
   189→            continue
   190→
   191→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   192→        zone = "production"
   193→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
   194→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath).value
   195→
   196→        neighbors: dict
   197→        if lang.dep_graph:
   198→            entry = dep_graph_lookup(lang.dep_graph, filepath)
   199→            imports_raw = entry.get("imports", set())
   200→            importers_raw = entry.get("importers", set())
   201→            importer_count_value = importer_count(entry)
   202→            neighbors = {
   203→                "imports": _rel_list(imports_raw),
   204→                "importers": _rel_list(importers_raw),
   205→                "importer_count": importer_count_value,
   206→            }
   207→        else:
   208→            neighbors = {}
   209→
   210→        file_requests.append(
   211→            {
   212→                "file": rpath,
   213→                "content": content,
   214→                "zone": zone,
   215→                "loc": len(content.splitlines()),
   216→                "neighbors": neighbors,
   217→                "existing_issues": get_file_issues(state, filepath),
   218→            }
   219→        )
   220→    return file_requests
   221→
   222→
   223→def prepare_holistic_review(
   224→    path: Path,
   225→    lang: object,
   226→    state: dict,
   227→    options: HolisticReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   228→) -> dict[str, object]:
   229→    """Prepare holistic review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict."""
   230→    resolved_options = options or HolisticReviewPrepareOptions()
   231→    return prepare_holistic_review_payload(
   232→        path,
   233→        lang,
   234→        state,
   235→        resolved_options,
   236→        is_file_cache_enabled_fn=is_file_cache_enabled,
   237→        enable_file_cache_fn=enable_file_cache,
   238→        disable_file_cache_fn=disable_file_cache,
   239→        build_holistic_context_fn=build_holistic_context,
   240→        build_review_context_fn=build_review_context,
   241→        load_dimensions_for_lang_fn=load_dimensions_for_lang,
   242→        resolve_dimensions_fn=resolve_dimensions,
   243→        get_lang_guidance_fn=get_lang_guidance,
   244→        build_investigation_batches_fn=_build_investigation_batches,
   245→        batch_concerns_fn=_batch_concerns,
   246→        filter_batches_to_dimensions_fn=_filter_batches_to_dimensions,
   247→        append_full_sweep_batch_fn=append_full_sweep_batch,
   248→        serialize_context_fn=serialize_context,
   249→        log_best_effort_failure_fn=log_best_effort_failure,
   250→        logger=logger,
   251→    )
```
