## Review Prompt System Cleanup Plan

### Current Architecture (What the Reviewer Sees)

The reviewer sees content from two sources simultaneously:

1. **Blind packet JSON** (read from disk) -- contains `system_prompt` field (~4000 words of scoring philosophy, rules, calibration examples, dimension anchors, output format, IMPORT GUARD)
2. **Inline batch prompt** (the rendered text in the prompt file) -- assembled by `render_batch_prompt()` or `_build_claude_launch_prompt()`

Additionally, `ensure_prompt_contract()` appends `global_prompt_contract()` text to the `system_prompt` before it goes into the blind packet. This means the blind packet's `system_prompt` already contains BOTH the dimensions.json system_prompt AND the global_prompt_contract text.

### Redundancy Map

Here is what is stated WHERE, showing duplications:

| Concept | dimensions.json system_prompt | global_prompt_contract() (appended to system_prompt) | render_scoring_frame() | render_task_requirements() | render_scan_evidence_note() | render_historical_focus() | render_mechanical_concern_signals() |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| "Navigation aid, not scoring evidence" | SCORING INDEPENDENCE paragraph | -- | "navigation aids -- starting points" | -- | "navigation aid, not scoring evidence" | "navigation aid, not scoring evidence" | "navigation aid, not scoring evidence" |
| "Do not default to 100" | Rule #11 | bullet point | -- | Rule 3b | -- | -- | -- |
| Score-issue consistency (<85 needs issue) | IMPORT GUARD | global_prompt_contract bullet | -- | Rule 4a | -- | -- | -- |
| Score <100 needs feedback | IMPORT GUARD | global_prompt_contract bullet | -- | (implied by rule 1) | -- | -- | -- |
| Score >85 needs issues_preventing_higher_score | -- | global_prompt_contract bullet | -- | Rule 9 | -- | -- | -- |
| Structural/root cause thinking | -- | global_prompt_contract paragraph | -- | Rule 1c | -- | -- | -- |
| "JSON only, no markdown" | OUTPUT FORMAT section | -- | -- | Rule 13 | -- | -- | -- |
| related_files requirement | Rule #2: "at least one entry" | -- | -- | Rule 5: "at least 2 files when possible" | -- | -- | -- |
| Issues must be defects only | Rules #8, #9, #10 | "Issues must always describe defects" | -- | -- | -- | -- | -- |
| Exploration guidance | system_prompt paragraph 2 | -- | render_scoring_frame para 2 | Rule 2/2a/2b | -- | -- | -- |
| Dimension boundaries guidance | -- | global_prompt_contract bullet | -- | Rule 2c | -- | -- | -- |
| "Do not suppress valid issues" | -- | global_prompt_contract bullet | -- | Rule 3a | -- | -- | -- |

### Conflicting Thresholds

- **system_prompt Rule #2**: "Every finding MUST include at least one entry in related_files as evidence."
- **render_task_requirements Rule 5**: "Every issue must include `related_files` with at least 2 files when possible."

These conflict. The system_prompt rule is the correct one: at least one is mandatory, more is encouraged but not required. The inline prompt's "at least 2 when possible" is a softened version that still contradicts.

### The Plan

#### Step 1: Absorb global_prompt_contract() into dimensions.json system_prompt

The `global_prompt_contract()` function restates rules already in the system_prompt, with a few additions that should be folded into the system_prompt directly:

**Already covered in system_prompt** (just remove from global_prompt_contract):
- "Do not default to 100" -- Rule #11
- "Do not suppress valid issues" -- covered by Rules #8, #9
- "Issues must always describe defects" -- Rules #8, #9, #10
- Scores below 85 need issues -- IMPORT GUARD

**Additions to absorb into system_prompt** (add to dimensions.json, then delete from contract):
- `issues_preventing_higher_score` requirement for scores >85 -- add as a new rule or extend IMPORT GUARD
- `root_cause_cluster` guidance -- add as a new rule
- Dimension boundary guidance ("if an issue spans dimensions, report under most impacted") -- add as a new rule

After this, `global_prompt_contract()` is deleted entirely, and `ensure_prompt_contract()` becomes a no-op that just returns its input.

**Files to change:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/review_data/dimensions.json` -- add the 3 missing rules to system_prompt
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/feedback_contract.py` -- delete `global_prompt_contract()`, make `ensure_prompt_contract()` a pass-through (or delete it and update callers)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/dimensions/data.py` -- remove `ensure_prompt_contract` calls from `load_dimensions()` and `load_dimensions_for_lang()`

#### Step 2: Gut render_task_requirements()

Current render_task_requirements has ~30 sub-items that heavily overlap the system_prompt. After Step 1, the system_prompt is the single source of truth. The inline prompt should only contain batch-specific instructions the reviewer cannot get from the blind packet.

**Replace with a short block:**
```
Task requirements:
1. Read the blind packet's `system_prompt` — it contains all scoring rules, calibration, and dimension anchors.
2. Start with the seed files above, then freely explore the repository.
3. Keep issues and scoring scoped to this batch's listed dimensions.
4. Respect scope controls in the blind packet config (exclude, suppress, zone overrides).
5. Return 0-{issues_cap} high-quality issues for this batch (empty array allowed).
{render_dimension_focus(dim_set)}
6. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.
```

The dimension-specific focus items (9a-9j) stay because they are batch-specific context about which evidence fields to consult. They just lose their "9a/9b" numbering and get renumbered.

**What gets removed** (because it restates system_prompt):
- Rule 1c (structural/root cause) -- already in system_prompt via Step 1
- Rules 3a, 3b (do not suppress, do not default to 100) -- system_prompt Rules #8-#12
- Rule 4, 4a (score/issue consistency, threshold) -- system_prompt IMPORT GUARD
- Rule 5 (related_files "at least 2") -- system_prompt Rule #2 says "at least one" (resolves the conflict)
- Rule 6 (issue fields) -- the output schema already shows required fields
- Rule 7 (impact_scope/fix_scope) -- the output schema shows these
- Rule 8 (dimension_notes) -- system_prompt already covers this via IMPORT GUARD
- Rule 9 (issues_preventing_higher_score) -- moved to system_prompt in Step 1
- Rule 10 (one decimal place) -- system_prompt ASSESSMENTS section
- Rule 11 (ignore prior context) -- system_prompt already says "do not anchor"
- Rule 12 (do not edit files) -- operational, keep in short form

**File to change:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py` -- rewrite `render_task_requirements()`

#### Step 3: Simplify render_scoring_frame()

Current `render_scoring_frame()` has two paragraphs:
1. YOUR TASK -- tells reviewer to read code and judge dimensions (useful batch-specific framing)
2. WHAT WE GIVE YOU AND WHY -- restates the "navigation aids, not scoring evidence" concept

Paragraph 2 is redundant with the system_prompt's SCORING INDEPENDENCE section AND with the contextual labels on each evidence section (render_historical_focus, render_mechanical_concern_signals, render_scan_evidence_note all say "navigation aid, not scoring evidence").

**Change:** Keep paragraph 1 (YOUR TASK), remove paragraph 2 (WHAT WE GIVE YOU AND WHY). The contextual labels on individual sections are sufficient. The system_prompt has the full philosophy.

**File to change:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py` -- trim `render_scoring_frame()`

#### Step 4: Simplify render_scan_evidence_note()

Currently says:
> "Mechanical scan evidence -- navigation aid, not scoring evidence: The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` with aggregated signals from all mechanical detectors..."

This is fine as-is. The "navigation aid, not scoring evidence" label is a contextual marker on this specific section, not a restatement of philosophy. The description of what `scan_evidence` contains is useful operational context. **Keep as-is.**

#### Step 5: Fix the related_files conflict in dimensions.json

In the system_prompt Rule #2, change "at least one entry in related_files" to be the canonical version. Since we are deleting the "at least 2 when possible" from render_task_requirements, the conflict resolves automatically. No change needed to dimensions.json here -- Rule #2 already says "at least one entry in related_files as evidence" which is correct.

#### Step 6: Update the output schema in _render_output_schema (batch path)

The output schema in `_render_output_schema()` in `prompt_template.py` is batch-specific (includes batch_index, batch name) and not duplicated in the system_prompt. **Keep as-is.**

The output schema in `_build_claude_launch_prompt()` in `external.py` is similarly session-specific. **Keep as-is.**

#### Step 7: Update _build_claude_launch_prompt (external path)

The external path in `external.py` calls the same shared render functions. After Steps 2-3, it automatically gets the simplified versions. However, the external path also has its own `session_requirements` block which includes "Return JSON only (no markdown fences)" -- this is fine because it's session-specific context (keep session.id, keep session.token).

Review what render calls it makes:
- `render_scoring_frame()` -- simplified in Step 3
- `render_scan_evidence_note()` -- kept as-is
- `render_task_requirements()` -- simplified in Step 2
- `render_scope_enums()` -- keep (short reference)

**No additional changes needed** beyond what Steps 2-3 provide.

#### Step 8: Update tests

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/review/import_scoring/test_review_external.py` -- assertions on lines 263-265 check that `render_scoring_frame()` and `render_scan_evidence_note()` appear in the prompt. These will still pass since those functions still exist, just with trimmed content. May need to update if the test checks exact string content.
- Any tests that import `global_prompt_contract` or `ensure_prompt_contract` need updating.

#### Step 9: Clean up feedback_contract.py exports

After removing `global_prompt_contract()` and simplifying `ensure_prompt_contract()`:
- Remove from `__all__`
- The constants (`LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD`, `HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD`, etc.) stay -- they are used by the validation/normalization code, not just prompt rendering
- The threshold-checking functions (`score_requires_dimension_issue`, `score_requires_explicit_feedback`) stay -- used by import validation

### Resulting Prompt Structure (After Cleanup)

**Blind packet system_prompt** (single source of truth for review philosophy):
- Scoring philosophy
- Scoring independence (navigation aids concept)
- All rules (including absorbed: root_cause_cluster, dimension boundaries, issues_preventing_higher_score)
- Calibration examples
- Output format
- Global anchors
- Dimension anchors
- IMPORT GUARD

**Inline batch prompt** (batch-specific operational context):
- Role/identity ("You are a focused subagent reviewer...")
- Batch metadata (repo root, packet path, batch index, dimensions, rationale)
- YOUR TASK framing (one paragraph)
- Scan evidence note (what `holistic_context.scan_evidence` contains)
- Seed files list
- Historical issues (if any, with "navigation aid" label)
- Mechanical concern signals (if any, with "navigation aid" label)
- Short task requirements (6 items: read system_prompt, start with seeds, scope to batch dims, respect excludes, issue cap, JSON only)
- Dimension-specific focus (which evidence fields to consult for specific dims in this batch)
- Scope enums reference
- Output schema (batch-specific JSON shape)

### Implementation Order

1. Add the 3 missing rules to dimensions.json system_prompt (root_cause_cluster, dimension boundaries, issues_preventing_higher_score >85)
2. Delete `global_prompt_contract()` and simplify/delete `ensure_prompt_contract()` in feedback_contract.py
3. Remove `ensure_prompt_contract` wrapping from data.py loaders
4. Rewrite `render_task_requirements()` to the short 6-item version
5. Trim `render_scoring_frame()` to paragraph 1 only
6. Update test assertions
7. Run test suite to verify
